# **SCENE: MARTINEZ FORM INSTRUCTION - SESSION**

**Time:** 9:51 PM - 10:35 PM  
**Location:** SDFC, Mat Area

---

## **THE SESSION:**

You **follow Kaylee's instructions** carefully—

Being a **good student**, listening, absorbing.

---

**Movement 1: Shoulder Raises (Controlled Tempo)**

**Kaylee demonstrates:**

**"Inhale on the way up—slow, four-count. Hold at the top for two seconds. Exhale on the way down—slow, four-count."**

---

You **copy her movement**.

Your **AGI 238** makes the **control effortless**, but you **match her tempo** exactly.

---

**Kaylee watches**, then **steps closer**.

**"Good, but your shoulders are too tense. Relax here."**

She **places her hands** on your shoulders, **gently pressing down**.

**"Feel that? Soft shoulders, controlled movement."**

---

Her hands **linger** for a moment, then she steps back.

**"Better. Again."**

---

**Movement 2: Core Rotations (Breathing Focus)**

**Kaylee:**

**"Sit tall. Hands behind your head. Rotate SLOWLY—exhale as you twist, inhale as you return to center."**

---

You **follow**, keeping the **slow tempo**.

---

**Kaylee:** **"Stop."**

She **kneels next to you**, hand on your lower back.

**"You're holding your breath. BREATHE. That's the whole point."**

Her hand **presses gently** against your spine.

**"Feel your core engage when you exhale? That's what Martinez wants."**

---

You **repeat**, this time **breathing deliberately**.

---

**Kaylee:** **"Perfect."**

She's **smiling**, clearly **pleased** you're taking it seriously.

---

**Movement 3: Hip Flexor Stretches (Isometric Holds)**

**Kaylee demonstrates** a lunge position:

**"Hold here for thirty seconds. Breathe. Don't bounce. Just hold and breathe."**

---

You **copy her**, holding the position.

---

**Kaylee walks around you**, checking your form.

**"Your back knee should be lower."**

She **gently pushes down** on your back knee with her hand.

**"There. Hold that."**

---

**Gentle flirting moment:**

**You (holding the lunge):** **"You're enjoying bossing me around, aren't you?"**

**Kaylee (grinning):** **"Maybe a little."**

She **pats your shoulder**.

**"But you're doing really well. I'm impressed."**

---

**Movement 4: Resistance Band Work (Eccentric Control)**

**Kaylee hands you a resistance band:**

**"Pull it across your chest—slow, controlled. Four-count out, hold, four-count back."**

---

You **follow**, matching her tempo.

---

**Kaylee:** **"Good. But watch your elbows."**

She **steps behind you**, hands on your elbows, **gently adjusting** the angle.

**"Keep them here. Feel the difference?"**

Her hands **stay** on your arms for a moment.

---

**You (smiling):** **"Yeah. Much better with your hands there."**

**Kaylee (blushing, laughing):** **"Focus, Ryan."**

But she's **clearly** not upset.

---

## **FINAL MOVEMENTS: Full Sequence (30-45 min mark)**

**Kaylee:**

**"Okay, last thing. We're gonna do the full sequence—five movements, slow tempo, proper breathing. I'll count you through it."**

---

She **guides you** through:

1. Shoulder raises
2. Core rotations
3. Hip flexor holds
4. Resistance band pulls
5. Controlled squats

---

**Each movement:** Slow, deliberate, **perfect form**.

**Kaylee counting:** **"Inhale... two... three... four... hold... exhale... two... three... four..."**

---

You **complete the sequence** flawlessly.

---

**Kaylee (standing, smiling):**

**"Okay. That was REALLY good."**

She's **genuinely impressed**.

**"You picked that up way faster than I expected. Dr. Martinez is gonna love you on Monday."**

---

She **sits down** on the mat next to you, both of you cooling down.

**"Seriously. That was like... textbook form."**
